- **pediatric-brain-tumor-seg-eval**: Evaluates deep learning architectures for multi-class segmentation of pediatric brain tumors on MRI scans. It probes the model's ability to accurately delineate tumor sub-regions (whole tumor, enhanced tumor, cystic component, edema) and assesses cross-domain generalizability to adult glioma data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on PED BraTS 2024, CBTN, BraTS Adult Glioma 2023, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports lesion-wise Dice. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pediatric-brain-tumor-seg-eval) · ★3)
- **pediatric-brain-tumor-wsi-eval**: This benchmark evaluates the ability of weakly supervised multiple instance learning models to classify pediatric brain tumors from whole-slide histopathology images. It probes fine-grained diagnostic discrimination across varying class granularities (2 to 7 classes) under conditions of class imbalance and limited data. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Pediatric brain tumor WSI dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Macro F1. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/pediatric-brain-tumor-wsi-eval) · ★3)
- **pediatric-fever-triage**: Use when a caregiver reports a child (age 0 to under 18) with fever or subjective warmth at night, with or without accompanying symptoms. Produces a three-way next-action decision — Call (emergency or hotline), Go (open pharmacy), or Wait (home self-care) — with the Yorukusu safety boundary on every output. Inputs include age, temperature (measured or subjective), fever duration, concurrent symptoms, antipyretic history, and the caller's Personal Health Context. Never outputs a diagnosis and never recommends prescription medication. ([Guttyo/yorukusu](https://github.com/Guttyo/yorukusu/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pediatric-fever-triage) · ★1)
- **pediatric-growth-reference-educator**: Explain the WHO Child Growth Standards and growth reference concepts for educational use. Educational and synthetic by design; outputs are drafts for review. ([salikkhann/openmedskills](https://github.com/salikkhann/openmedskills/tree/HEAD/dist/agent-skills/pediatric-growth-reference-educator) · ★5)
- **pediatric-obesity-consultation**: Guide a non-stigmatising pediatric obesity consultation using the 5As framework (Ask, Assess, Advise, Agree, Assist) and 4Ms assessment (Metabolic, Mechanical, Mental Health, Social Milieu), based on the CMAJ 2025 guideline. Trigger when a clinician asks how to approach a weight conversation with a child or family, how to use the 5As for obesity, how to assess a child with obesity, or how to conduct a non-judgmental obesity visit. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-consultation) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-etiology-screener**: Screen a child with obesity for red flags suggesting monogenic, syndromic, or secondary endocrine causes — distinguishing atypical from typical exogenous obesity. Trigger when a clinician asks whether obesity could be genetic or hormonal, suspects Prader-Willi syndrome, Cushing disease, hypothyroidism, ROHHAD, Bardet-Biedl syndrome, leptin deficiency, or craniopharyngioma, or when a child has early-onset severe obesity, short stature with obesity, hyperphagia, hypotonia, dysmorphic features, or developmental delay with weight gain. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-etiology-screener) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-intervention-selector**: Select the right treatment tier for a child with obesity — behavioural, pharmacologic, or surgical — using the CMAJ 2025 pediatric obesity guideline. Trigger when a clinician asks what to do for a child with obesity, which intervention to start, whether to escalate treatment, or when to consider medication or surgery for pediatric obesity. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-intervention-selector) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-selector**: Select the right pharmacologic agent for a child aged 12+ with obesity using the CMAJ 2025 guideline — choosing between GLP-1 receptor agonists, metformin, or orlistat with monitoring guidance. Trigger when a clinician asks which medication to use for pediatric obesity, whether to start semaglutide or metformin in a child, or how to manage obesity pharmacologically in adolescents. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-pharmacotherapy-selector) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-obesity-surgical-screener**: Screen a child aged 13+ with obesity for eligibility for bariatric surgery (laparoscopic sleeve gastrectomy or Roux-en-Y gastric bypass) using the CMAJ 2025 guideline. Trigger when a clinician asks whether a child qualifies for weight loss surgery, bariatric surgery in adolescents, sleeve gastrectomy in a teenager, or when to refer for surgical management of pediatric obesity. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pediatric-obesity/pediatric-obesity-surgical-screener) · ★14 · 4 copies)
- **pediatric-optometry**: Pediatric Visual Development & Amblyopia: Preferential looking tests (Teller acuity cards), cycloplegic refraction, strabismus detection, amblyopia patching regimens, and refractive error tracking. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/optometry/pediatric-optometry))
- **pediatric-ot**: Pediatric Occupational Therapy & Milestone Tracking: Peabody Developmental Motor Scales (PDMS-2), fine motor coordination, grasp pattern development, visual-motor integration (Beery VMI), and school-based OT intervention. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/occupational-therapy/pediatric-ot))
- **pediatric-speech**: Early Childhood Speech & Language Milestones: Late talker identification, parent-implemented language interventions (Hanen model), emergent literacy facilitation, and developmental sound acquisition tracking. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/speech-language-pathology/pediatric-speech))
- **pediatric-surgeon**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a pediatric general surgeon — triaging bilious vomiting or an acute abdomen in a neonate, deciding operative timing and staged-versus-definitive repair for congenital anomalies (malrotation, gastroschisis, CDH, biliary atresia), managing necrotizing enterocolitis by clinical stage, or working an acute scrotum against a salvage-time clock. US pediatric surgical practice (APSA guidelines). A reasoning aid, not medical advice. ([wonsukchoi/domain-experts](https://github.com/wonsukchoi/domain-experts/tree/HEAD/roles/pediatric-surgeon) · ★27)
- **pediatric_drug_safety**: Pediatric Drug Safety Review - Evaluate pediatric drug safety: pediatric use info, child safety, dosage forms, and overdosage info from FDA. Use this skill for pediatric pharmacology tasks involving get pediatric use info by drug name get child safety info by drug name get dosage forms and strengths by drug name get overdosage info by drug name. Combines 4 tools from 1 SCP server(s). ([SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw](https://github.com/SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pediatric_drug_safety) · ★399 · 3 copies)
- **pediatrician**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a general outpatient pediatrician — triaging a sick-child complaint by age and risk, structuring a well-child visit against the periodicity schedule, deciding whether a fever or growth-curve change warrants workup versus reassurance, or navigating a vaccine-hesitant parent conversation. US primary-care pediatrics (AAP/CDC guidelines). A reasoning aid, not medical advice. ([wonsukchoi/domain-experts](https://github.com/wonsukchoi/domain-experts/tree/HEAD/roles/pediatrician) · ★27)
- **pediatrics**: &gt; ([NeuralBlitz/Mito](https://github.com/NeuralBlitz/Mito/tree/HEAD/.opencode/skills/pediatrics) · 2 copies)
- **pediatrics-calculators**: Use when applying pediatric decision rules or growth/weight-based calculations. Includes PECARN head injury rule, CDC/WHO growth standards, weight-based dosing rules, Holliday-Segar maintenance fluids, and Parkland burn formula. ([rhavekost/clinical-toolkit](https://github.com/rhavekost/clinical-toolkit/tree/HEAD/dist/consumer/claude/pediatrics-calculators) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **pedido**: Estrutura um pedido de programação do NOTAS PM em contexto, objetivo, escopo, forma de verificar e ação permitida, completando só o que faltar. Use quando o usuário digitar /pedido, ou quando o pedido for vago e de risco (exclusão, repasse, sincronização, banco, publicação em produção). ([cleitonjussara-coder/cleitonjussara-coder.io](https://github.com/cleitonjussara-coder/cleitonjussara-coder.io/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pedido) · ★1)

Use depois do contrato assinado e das GRUs, ou quando o operador disser "fazer o deposito", "registrar a marca no INPI", "monta o pedido no e-Marcas", "depositar a marca", "preencher o e-Marcas", "/deposito-marca". Cabe arresto (art. 830), indisponibilidade de bens via SISBAJUD (art. 854), bloqueio de veiculos (RENAJUD), protesto extrajudicial preventivo. Exige FUMUS BONI IURIS + PERICULUM IN MORA. Use quando ha risco de dilapidacao patrimonial ou de prescricao iminente. ([sbroggioadv/execucao-adv-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/execucao-adv-os-marketplace/tree/HEAD/execucao-adv-os/skills/pedido-tutela-urgencia) · ★1)
- **pedido-uniformizacao**: Elabora pedido de uniformização ao Conselho Pleno do CRSS quando há divergência entre Câmaras sobre tese previdenciária. Skill técnica específica — exige demonstração da divergência + tese a uniformizar. Pode ser usado em paralelo a recurso à Câmara (preliminar de uniformização). Use sempre que o advogado disser: "uniformização", "divergência entre câmaras", "conselho pleno", "tese uniforme", "pedido uniformização CRSS", "afetação ao pleno". Não use pra recurso comum (use [[recurso-camara-julgamento]]). ([raphaelRPC/dr-claudio](https://github.com/raphaelRPC/dr-claudio/tree/HEAD/familias/dr-claudio/dr-claudio-previdenciario-administrativo/skills/pedido-uniformizacao) · ★11)

- **pedidos**: Ver pedidos, cotizar el envío, confirmar contraentregas de riesgo, generar o cancelar la guía y resolver lo que el agente dejó esperando aprobación. - **pedidos**: Gestión completa de pedidos pizzepos. Dos tipos: 'pos' (con cuenta_id, items incremental, enviado a cocina) y 'tienda' (plano, todos los items de una, cliente_nombre como ancla de recogida). Bridge desde comandero (comandero.enviar_cocina → pedido formal). 12 tools + 12 ui_handlers. ([Noninapizzicas/3enki](https://github.com/Noninapizzicas/3enki/tree/HEAD/modules/cosecha/cantera/enki/pizzepos-pedidos))

Use before claiming pedigree or animal-model support. ([itchyshin/HSquared.jl](https://github.com/itchyshin/HSquared.jl/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/pedigree-ainv-review) · ★1)
- **pedigree-analysis**: 種牡馬一覧と、登録済み統計がある場合は芝ダ・距離カテゴリ別の勝利数・出走数・勝率を表示する。「血統分析」「種牡馬の成績を見たい」といった依頼で発動する。 ([sogengineer/arima-analy](https://github.com/sogengineer/arima-analy/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/pedigree-analysis) · ★4)
- **pedigree-kinship-matrix-preparation**: Use when RNA-seq samples come from related individuals (family/pedigree cohort) and you want to model pairwise genetic relatedness as a covariance random effect, or to compare kinship vs non-kinship model fit via per-gene AIC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/pedigree-kinship-matrix-preparation))
- **pedigree-sv-genotype-concordance-evaluation**: Use when you have HiFi WGS read sets for multiple related samples (e.g. a pedigree like CEPH-1463) and need to compare SV callers by genotype concordance with known inheritance rather than a truth-set benchmark. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pedigree-sv-genotype-concordance-evaluation))
- **pedigree-trio-genotype-analysis**: Use when a parent–parent–child trio of haplotype-resolved long-read alignments is available and you need to flag each genotyped tandem repeat as Mendelian consistent or inconsistent, as done for the HG00731/HG00732/HG00733 Puerto Rican PB trio. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/pedigree-trio-genotype-analysis))
- **pedimento-matcher**: Cotejar Previo (fotos) contra Factura (Partidas) y Proforma/Pedimento (Secciones) en despacho aduanal mexicano, y generar un reporte de discrepancias con evidencia. Usar cuando el usuario pida validar, cotejar o auditar una Proforma/Pedimento contra su Factura y fotos de Previo. ([AngelMaldonado/pedimento-matcher](https://github.com/AngelMaldonado/pedimento-matcher/tree/HEAD/pedimento-matcher))
- **pedimento-tracker**: Lleva un registro local de pedimentos pendientes y despachados - clave de pedimento (A1, IN, AF, F4, F5, V1), agente aduanal y patente, aduana, semáforo, plazos de retorno IMMEX y documentos del expediente electrónico. Usar cuando el usuario diga pedimento tracker, seguimiento de pedimentos, estatus de mi importación, plazos de importación temporal, control de pedimentos, qué clave de pedimento uso. - **pedir-aprovacao**: Procedimento para pedir aprovação ao mantenedor do Artifício RPG — o formato do bloco "APROVAÇÃO NECESSÁRIA", o que cada campo precisa conter, e a mecânica de worktree e de pacote apt/lib nova. Use quando for pedir autorização para commit, merge, deploy, escrita na VM, SQL write, mudança de DNS/tunnel, criação de worktree ou instalação de pacote/biblioteca nova — ou quando um gate bloquear uma ação e mandar pedir aprovação. A LISTA do que exige aprovação fica no `AGENTS.md` §Autorização, não aqui; aqui está só o como pedir.
